- **economy-auditor**: Audits a game economy, progression or gacha configuration by running the plugin's simulators and reporting inflation, walls, outliers and pity problems with exact numbers. ([VRhino/Conquest_prototype](https://github.com/VRhino/Conquest_prototype/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ecs-component-creator.md))

([VRhino/Conquest_prototype](https://github.com/VRhino/Conquest_prototype/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/ecs-flow-debugger.md))

- **effort-grader**: Blind grader for effortmining. Grades one artifact against a task prompt and a fixed rubric ONLY.
